import urllib.request
import json

yoe_keywords = [
    "years of experience", "yoe", "years' experience", "minimum 5 years",
    "minimum 6 years", "minimum 7 years", "minimum 8 years", "minimum 10 years"
]

files = [
    r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\lever_systems_jobs.json",
    r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\deep_crawl_systems_pool.json",
    r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\indian_infra_jobs.json"
]

all_jobs = []
for fpath in files:
    try:
        with open(fpath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
            for comp in data:
                cname = comp.get("company", "")
                for r in comp.get("roles", []):
                    all_jobs.append({
                        "company": cname,
                        "title": r.get("title", ""),
                        "location": r.get("location", ""),
                        "url": r.get("url", "")
                    })
    except Exception:
        pass

# Deduplicate
seen = set()
unique_jobs = []
for j in all_jobs:
    key = (j["company"], j["title"])
    if key not in seen:
        seen.add(key)
        unique_jobs.append(j)

print(f"Total unique systems/infra roles extracted: {len(unique_jobs)}")
for j in unique_jobs[:15]:
    print(f"- [{j['company']}] {j['title']} ({j['location']})")

out_file = r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\consolidated_systems_crawl.json"
with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(unique_jobs, f, indent=2)

print(f"\nSaved consolidated {len(unique_jobs)} roles to {out_file}")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
TENSIX Multi-Source Autonomous Outreach Engine
Sources: Y Combinator (HN Algolia API) + Product Hunt (Daily Ingestion) + Betalist
Pipeline:
1. Harvests live startup domains from multi-source endpoints.
2. Reputation Shield: Validates live HTTPS, catches cross-domain hijacks and parked scams.
3. 21-Variable Multi-Layer Scorer: Evaluates RFC 7208 lookups, DMARC, and Vendor Sprawl.
4. Threshold Gate: Bleed Score >= 60.
5. Autonomous Dispatch: Delivers personalized diagnostic teardown via Brevo SMTP (hemal@tensix.in).
6. Conservative Throttle: 60-120s jitter, max 5-10 dispatches per cycle.
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import time
import random
import smtplib
import urllib.request
import urllib.parse
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart

sys.path.append(r"D:\projects\HK\crawler")
from mailops_scorer import score_and_package_domain
from reputation_shield import verify_live_website

DISPATCH_LOG_FILE = r"D:\projects\HK\tensix\data\autonomous_dispatch_log.json"

def load_env(env_path: str = r"D:\projects\HK\.env"):
    config = {}
    if os.path.exists(env_path):
        with open(env_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                line = line.strip()
                if line and not line.startswith("#") and "=" in line:
                    k, v = line.split("=", 1)
                    config[k.strip()] = v.strip()
    return config

def get_already_dispatched():
    if os.path.exists(DISPATCH_LOG_FILE):
        try:
            with open(DISPATCH_LOG_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                return set(json.load(f))
        except:
            return set()
    return set()

def record_dispatch(domain: str):
    dispatched = get_already_dispatched()
    dispatched.add(domain)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(DISPATCH_LOG_FILE), exist_ok=True)
    with open(DISPATCH_LOG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(list(dispatched), f, indent=2)

def harvest_yc_candidates(queries=["Launch YC", "YC W24", "YC S24"]):
    headers = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) TENSIX-MultiHarvester/1.0"}
    discovered = {}
    print(f"[*] [1/3] Harvesting YC cohorts across: {queries}")
    
    for q in queries:
        url = f"https://hn.algolia.com/api/v1/search?query={urllib.parse.quote(q)}&tags=story&hitsPerPage=60"
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=8) as resp:
                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                for h in data.get("hits", []):
                    story_url = h.get("url") or ""
                    title = h.get("title") or ""
                    if story_url:
                        parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(story_url)
                        netloc = parsed.netloc.lower().split(":")[0]
                        parts = netloc.split(".")
                        if len(parts) >= 2:
                            root = ".".join(parts[-2:])
                            ignored = ("github.com", "medium.com", "youtube.com", "ycombinator.com", "techcrunch.com", "twitter.com", "x.com", "notion.site", "google.com")
                            if root not in ignored and root not in discovered:
                                discovered[root] = {"domain": root, "title": title, "source": "YC"}
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[-] Error querying YC: {e}")
    return list(discovered.values())

def harvest_producthunt_rss():
    print("[*] [2/3] Harvesting Product Hunt launches via RSS feed...")
    url = "https://www.producthunt.com/feed"
    headers = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)"}
    candidates = {}
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=8) as resp:
            content = resp.read().decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
            # Extract outbound links
            links = re.findall(r'<link>(https?://[^<]+)</link>', content)
            for l in links:
                parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(l)
                netloc = parsed.netloc.lower().split(":")[0]
                parts = netloc.split(".")
                if len(parts) >= 2:
                    root = ".".join(parts[-2:])
                    if root not in ("producthunt.com", "google.com", "apple.com"):
                        candidates[root] = {"domain": root, "title": "Product Hunt Launch", "source": "ProductHunt"}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[-] Note: Product Hunt RSS direct fetch skipped: {e}")
    return list(candidates.values())

def resolve_target_inbox(domain: str, title: str):
    """Generates canonical executive/founder outreach targets."""
    # Pattern: founders@, founder first name, or leadership
    primary = f"founders@{domain}"
    cc = [f"team@{domain}"]
    name = "Founders"
    
    # Check for known founder signatures in title
    if " by " in title:
        author_candidate = title.split(" by ")[-1].strip().split()[0]
        if author_candidate.isalpha() and len(author_candidate) > 2:
            name = author_candidate
            primary = f"{author_candidate.lower()}@{domain}"
            cc = [f"founders@{domain}"]

    return primary, cc, name

def dispatch_teardown(domain: str, profile: dict, title: str):
    config = load_env()
    smtp_login = config.get("BREVO_SMTP_LOGIN")
    smtp_key = config.get("BREVO_SMTP_KEY")
    sender_email = config.get("SENDER_EMAIL", "hemal@tensix.in")
    sender_name = config.get("SENDER_NAME", "Hemal Shah | TENSIX")

    to_email, cc_emails, recipient_name = resolve_target_inbox(domain, title)
    
    spf_lookups = profile["spf_lookups"]
    dmarc_policy = profile["dmarc_policy"]
    vendors = profile["detected_vendors"]

    if spf_lookups > 10:
        subject = f"RFC 7208 PermError on {domain} primary sending domain ({spf_lookups} DNS lookups)"
        flaw_desc = f"Your primary root SPF record evaluates to {spf_lookups} recursive DNS lookups, which exceeds the RFC 7208 Section 4.6.4 ceiling of 10 across {len(vendors)} SaaS tools ({', '.join(vendors[:4])})."
        impact_desc = f"Receiving MTAs at Google Workspace, Yahoo, and Microsoft 365 terminate SPF evaluation at lookup 11 and flag an immediate PermError. Combined with your DMARC policy ({dmarc_policy}), automated outbound and transactional messages are at continuous risk of being silently discarded or routed into spam."
    else:
        subject = f"Missing DMARC policy & SPF configuration flags on {domain}"
        flaw_desc = f"Your sending domain currently operates with an un-enforced DMARC configuration (p={dmarc_policy}) and zero configured forensic RUA reporting mailboxes."
        impact_desc = "Under Google & Yahoo's 2024 mandatory sender guidelines, unauthenticated domains face strict spam penalties on transactional pipelines and have zero defense against brand spoofing."

    text_body = f"""Hi {recipient_name},

I hope this note finds you well.

I am an infrastructure and protocol engineer specializing in RFC transport compliance (tensix.in). While conducting automated audits across recent high-growth SaaS launches, I identified a critical DNS configuration error on {domain} that directly impacts your email delivery.

Technical Diagnostic:
{flaw_desc}

Impact on Deliverability:
{impact_desc}

Remediation Architecture:
We deconstruct third-party vendor includes and compile an aggregated, static CIDR network map that condenses your entire lookup footprint down to <= 3 lookups with zero workflow downtime.

Our one-time triage and hardening engagement is a flat $350 (or ₹30,000). 
Once confirmed, we deliver the complete, verified DNS block, coordinate with your team during the record swap, and execute live end-to-end inbox delivery verification across Google and Microsoft within 2 hours.

I have already generated the required DNS mapping. Let me know if you would like me to get this deployed for you today.

Best regards,

Hemal Shah
Systems & RFC Protocol Forensics | TENSIX
https://tensix.in
"""

    html_body = f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<body style="font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.6; color: #1a1a1a;">
<p>Hi {recipient_name},</p>

<p>I hope this note finds you well.</p>

<p>I am an infrastructure and protocol engineer specializing in RFC transport compliance (<a href="https://tensix.in" style="color: #2563eb; text-decoration: none;">tensix.in</a>). While conducting automated audits across recent high-growth SaaS launches, I identified a critical DNS configuration error on <strong>{domain}</strong> that directly impacts your email delivery.</p>

<h4 style="color: #b91c1c; margin-bottom: 4px;">Technical Diagnostic:</h4>
<p style="margin-top: 0;">{flaw_desc}</p>

<h4 style="color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px;">Impact on Deliverability:</h4>
<p style="margin-top: 0;">{impact_desc}</p>

<h4 style="color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px;">Remediation Architecture:</h4>
<p style="margin-top: 0;">We deconstruct third-party vendor includes and compile an aggregated, static CIDR network map that condenses your entire lookup footprint down to &lt;= 3 lookups with zero workflow downtime.</p>

<div style="background-color: #f8fafc; border-left: 4px solid #2563eb; padding: 12px 16px; margin: 16px 0;">
  <p style="margin: 0; font-weight: 600; color: #0f172a;">Commercial Scope ($350 Flat Fee):</p>
  <p style="margin: 4px 0 0 0; font-size: 14px; color: #475569;">Our one-time triage engagement is a flat <strong>$350</strong> (or &#8377;30,000). We deliver the complete validated DNS block, coordinate with your team, and execute live end-to-end inbox delivery verification across Google and Microsoft within 2 hours.</p>
</div>

<p>I have already generated the required DNS mapping. Let me know if you would like me to get this deployed for you today.</p>

<p>Best regards,</p>

<p><strong>Hemal Shah</strong><br>
Systems & RFC Protocol Forensics | <a href="https://tensix.in" style="color: #2563eb; text-decoration: none;">TENSIX</a></p>
</body>
</html>"""

    msg = MIMEMultipart("alternative")
    msg["From"] = f"{sender_name} <{sender_email}>"
    msg["To"] = to_email
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg.attach(MIMEText(text_body, "plain"))
    msg.attach(MIMEText(html_body, "html"))

    all_recipients = [to_email] + cc_emails

    try:
        server = smtplib.SMTP("smtp-relay.brevo.com", 587)
        server.starttls()
        server.login(smtp_login, smtp_key)
        server.sendmail(sender_email, all_recipients, msg.as_string())
        server.quit()
        print(f"[+] [DISPATCH SUCCESS] {domain} -> {all_recipients}")
        record_dispatch(domain)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[-] [DISPATCH FAILED] {domain}: {e}")
        return False

def run_autonomous_workflow(max_dispatches_per_run: int = 5):
    print("=" * 80)
    print("TENSIX AUTONOMOUS MULTI-SOURCE HARVEST & OUTREACH ENGINE")
    print(f"Enforcing 21-Variable Heuristic Weights + Reputation Shield + $350 Paywall")
    print("=" * 80)

    already_done = get_already_dispatched()
    print(f"[*] Found {len(already_done)} historically dispatched domains in ledger.")

    yc_pool = harvest_yc_candidates()
    ph_pool = harvest_producthunt_rss()
    total_pool = yc_pool + ph_pool

    # Deduplicate
    unique_candidates = {}
    for c in total_pool:
        d = c["domain"]
        if d not in unique_candidates and d not in already_done:
            unique_candidates[d] = c

    print(f"\n[*] Total fresh, uncontacted startup domains to audit: {len(unique_candidates)}")

    dispatched_count = 0

    for idx, (domain, meta) in enumerate(unique_candidates.items()):
        if dispatched_count >= max_dispatches_per_run:
            print(f"\n[!] Reached conservative run cap of {max_dispatches_per_run} dispatches. Throttling for reputation safety.")
            break

        print(f"\n---> Auditing [{idx+1}/{len(unique_candidates)}]: {domain} ({meta['source']})")

        # GATE 1: REPUTATION SHIELD (Liveness, Anti-hijack, Anti-slot)
        is_live, op_weight, shield_meta = verify_live_website(domain, timeout=5)
        if not is_live:
            print(f"     [REJECTED BY SHIELD]: {shield_meta.get('reason')}")
            continue

        print(f"     [SHIELD PASSED]: Operational Weight {op_weight}/100")

        # GATE 2: 21-VARIABLE DNS & SPRAWL SCORER
        profile = score_and_package_domain(domain, biz_type="High-Growth SaaS")
        bleed_score = profile["bleed_score"]
        print(f"     [BLEED SCORE]: {bleed_score}/100 | Lookups: {profile['spf_lookups']}/10 | DMARC: {profile['dmarc_policy']}")

        if bleed_score < 60:
            print("     [SKIPPED]: Bleed score < 60 (Healthy system)")
            continue

        print(f"     [ACTIONABLE TARGET CONFIRMED] Bleed Score: {bleed_score} >= 60")
        
        # GATE 3: AUTONOMOUS DISPATCH
        success = dispatch_teardown(domain, profile, meta["title"])
        if success:
            dispatched_count += 1
            # Rate-limiting jitter: 45 to 75 seconds between emails to keep Brevo score pristine
            if dispatched_count < max_dispatches_per_run:
                jitter = random.randint(45, 75)
                print(f"[*] [THROTTLE JITTER] Sleeping for {jitter}s to protect domain reputation...")
                time.sleep(jitter)

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print(f"[+] WORKFLOW CYCLE COMPLETE!")
    print(f"[+] Total Dispatches Sent This Run: {dispatched_count}")
    print(f"[+] Cumulative Dispatched in Ledger: {len(get_already_dispatched())}")
    print("=" * 80)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_autonomous_workflow(max_dispatches_per_run=5)

import json

with open(r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\massive_indian_jobs_harvest.json", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    massive = json.load(f)

with open(r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\india_specific_systems_jobs.json", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    india_jobs = json.load(f)

combined = []
seen = set()

for j in massive + india_jobs:
    title = j.get("title", "")
    comp = j.get("company", "")
    loc = j.get("location", "")
    url = j.get("url", "")
    key = (comp.lower(), title.lower())
    if key not in seen:
        seen.add(key)
        combined.append({
            "company": comp,
            "title": title,
            "location": loc,
            "url": url
        })

print(f"Total Unique Verified Indian Systems Roles: {len(combined)}\n")

by_company = {}
for j in combined:
    by_company.setdefault(j["company"], []).append(j)

for c, rlist in sorted(by_company.items(), key=lambda x: len(x[1]), reverse=True):
    print(f"=== {c} ({len(rlist)} systems roles) ===")
    for r in rlist:
        print(f"  • {r['title']} | {r['location']}")
        print(f"    URL: {r['url']}")
    print()

out_dest = r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\all_indian_systems_engineering_roles.json"
with open(out_dest, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(combined, f, indent=2)

print(f"Saved complete inventory to {out_dest}")

import json
import re

def analyze_and_cluster():
    with open(r'D:\projects\HK\crawler\betalist_startups.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        startups = json.load(f)
        
    # Categories definition with keyword heuristics
    categories = {
        "AI Agents & Automation Infrastructure": {
            "keywords": ["agent", "agents", "mcp", "automate", "automation", "kalshi", "coding agent", "receptionist", "deskbot", "tally", "muster", "sickle", "call4me"],
            "seed_multiple": 10, # Typical SaaS ARR multiple
            "base_valuation_k": 3500, # $3.5M Seed baseline
            "projected_5y_growth": "18x - 30x",
            "market_driver": "Rapid migration from static prompt wrappers to autonomous OS/MCP execution runtimes."
        },
        "AI Developer Tools & Code Reviews": {
            "keywords": ["code", "developer", "api", "review", "pull request", "heydeer", "transcription", "convert.express", "convert pdf"],
            "seed_multiple": 8,
            "base_valuation_k": 2800,
            "projected_5y_growth": "12x - 20x",
            "market_driver": "Developer productivity enhancement and automated CI/CD code gatekeeping."
        },
        "Autonomous SEO & Growth Engines": {
            "keywords": ["seo", "openseo", "tadaseo", "rankinai", "citations", "google rankings", "visibility", "marketing"],
            "seed_multiple": 6,
            "base_valuation_k": 2200,
            "projected_5y_growth": "8x - 15x",
            "market_driver": "Disruption of traditional SEMrush/Ahrefs monopolies via open-source and generative search indexing."
        },
        "Media Generation & Creative AI": {
            "keywords": ["3d", "video", "icons", "cinematic", "seedance", "quady3d", "kiriframe", "ai shorts", "cullit", "roomstaging", "photos"],
            "seed_multiple": 7,
            "base_valuation_k": 2500,
            "projected_5y_growth": "10x - 22x",
            "market_driver": "High-throughput 3D mesh and synthetic cinematic short-form video generation."
        },
        "B2B Vertical Workspaces & Management": {
            "keywords": ["hoa", "store", "headoffis", "basicops", "rentalthread", "care", "membership", "retello", "storage", "charging", "ev"],
            "seed_multiple": 5,
            "base_valuation_k": 2000,
            "projected_5y_growth": "6x - 12x",
            "market_driver": "Digitization of neglected offline brick-and-mortar operations (HOA, EV chargers, rentals)."
        },
        "Fintech, Budgeting & Split Billing": {
            "keywords": ["calendar", "budget", "cash flow", "split", "subsplit", "dividends", "nimblewit", "stocks", "cyrontek"],
            "seed_multiple": 8,
            "base_valuation_k": 3000,
            "projected_5y_growth": "9x - 18x",
            "market_driver": "Micro-fintech tackling irregular freelance gig-income and automated multi-tenant bill splitting."
        }
    }
    
    clustered = {cat: [] for cat in categories}
    unclassified = []
    
    for s in startups:
        text = (s['name'] + " " + s['slug'] + " " + s['pitch']).lower()
        matched = False
        for cat, meta in categories.items():
            if any(k in text for k in meta['keywords']):
                clustered[cat].append(s)
                matched = True
                break
        if not matched:
            unclassified.append(s)
            
    # Compile valuation report
    report = []
    for cat, items in clustered.items():
        meta = categories[cat]
        count = len(items)
        if count == 0: continue
        
        # Current Estimated Aggregate Pre-Seed / Seed Valuation (assuming early beta/traction $1.5M - $4M each)
        est_current_total_m = round((count * meta['base_valuation_k']) / 1000, 2)
        # 5-Year Projected Category Market Cap / Exit Value (with power law: 1-2 winners)
        min_mult = int(meta['projected_5y_growth'].split('x')[0].strip())
        max_mult = int(meta['projected_5y_growth'].split('-')[1].replace('x','').strip())
        proj_low_m = round(est_current_total_m * (min_mult * 0.35), 1) # Accounting for 65% failure rate
        proj_high_m = round(est_current_total_m * (max_mult * 0.50), 1)
        
        report.append({
            "category": cat,
            "startup_count": count,
            "startups": [f"{it['name']} ({it['betalist_url']})" for it in items],
            "current_est_aggregate_networth_usd": f"${est_current_total_m}M",
            "avg_seed_valuation_per_startup": f"${meta['base_valuation_k']/1000}M",
            "five_year_projected_category_cap": f"${proj_low_m}M - ${proj_high_m}M",
            "power_law_growth_multiple": meta['projected_5y_growth'],
            "market_driver": meta['market_driver']
        })
        
    out_file = r'D:\projects\HK\crawler\betalist_category_valuation_matrix.json'
    with open(out_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as fp:
        json.dump(report, fp, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        
    print(f"[+] Successfully clustered {len(startups)} startups into {len(report)} categories!")
    print(f"[+] Output written to {out_file}\n")
    
    # Print clean terminal report
    for r in report:
        print("=" * 85)
        print(f"CATEGORY: {r['category']} ({r['startup_count']} Startups)")
        print(f"Current Aggregate Seed Value: {r['current_est_aggregate_networth_usd']} | 5-Year Projected Category Cap: {r['five_year_projected_category_cap']}")
        print(f"Growth Multiple: {r['power_law_growth_multiple']} | Driver: {r['market_driver']}")
        print("Startups in this cluster:")
        for s in r['startups'][:6]:
            print(f"  • {s}")
        if len(r['startups']) > 6:
            print(f"  ...and {len(r['startups']) - 6} more.")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    analyze_and_cluster()

import urllib.request
import json

url = 'https://api.github.com/repos/garrytan/gstack/pulls?state=all'
req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'})
try:
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:
        prs = json.loads(resp.read().decode('utf-8'))
        for p in prs:
            user = p.get('user', {}).get('login')
            if user == 'morluto':
                print(f"PR #{p['number']}: {p['title']} ({p['state']})")
                print(f"Created: {p['created_at']}")
                print(f"Body: {p.get('body', '')[:300]}")
except Exception as ex:
    print('Error:', ex)

FILE: combine_files.py

import os
import glob

base_dir = r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler"
output_file = os.path.join(base_dir, "ALL_FILES_COMBINED.txt")

# Collect all Python and JSON files
py_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(base_dir, "*.py")))
json_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(base_dir, "*.json")))
all_files = py_files + json_files

with open(output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
    out.write(f"=== TOTAL COMBINED FILES: {len(all_files)} ===\n")
    out.write(f"Python Files: {len(py_files)} | JSON Files: {len(json_files)}\n\n")
    
    for f in all_files:
        if os.path.abspath(f) == os.path.abspath(output_file):
            continue
        fname = os.path.basename(f)
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write(f"FILE: {fname}\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")
        try:
            with open(f, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as infile:
                out.write(infile.read())
        except Exception as e:
            out.write(f"ERROR READING FILE: {e}\n")
        out.write("\n\n")

print(f"Successfully combined {len(all_files)} files into: {output_file}")
print(f"File size: {os.path.getsize(output_file)} bytes")


================================================================================
FILE: connected_crawler.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
TENSIX DEEP RECURSIVE PIPELINE CRAWLER & AUDIT ENGINE (v6.0 Enterprise)
=======================================================================
Fully automated, end-to-end 5-Stage Pipeline with true recursive SPF tree resolution:
Stage 1: Reputation & Liveness Shield (reputation_shield)
Stage 2: Deep BFS Web Crawler (HTML /team, /about, /terms, /privacy, /careers)
Stage 3: Subdomain & Burner Reconnaissance (Recursive lookup counting on subdomains, detecting VAR_28, VAR_29, and nested loops)
Stage 4: Mathematical 34-Variable & Weights Evaluation (Evaluates every variable deterministically)
Stage 5: Hardened Pre-Flight Socket Handshake RCPT Verifier (Rejects catch-all false positives)

INVARIANT: ZERO email dispatch without user explicit command.
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import re
import socket
import urllib.request
import urllib.parse
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from collections import deque
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Any, Tuple

BASE_DIR = r"D:\projects\HK\crawler"
if BASE_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, BASE_DIR)

from reputation_shield import verify_live_website
from smtp_rcpt_verifier import verify_email_comprehensive, simulate_smtp_handshake

def query_doh(name: str, qtype: str = "TXT") -> List[str]:
    url = f"https://cloudflare-dns.com/dns-query?name={name}&type={qtype}"
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"Accept": "application/dns-json"})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
            return [a.get("data") for a in data.get("Answer", [])]
    except Exception:
        return []

def trace_recursive_spf(record: str, depth: int = 0, max_depth: int = 5, seen: Optional[Set[str]] = None) -> Tuple[int, List[str], List[str]]:
    """
    Recursively counts DNS lookups and detects deprecated mechanisms (e.g. ptr, include loops).
    Returns: (total_lookups, detected_vendors, warnings)
    """
    if seen is None:
        seen = set()
    
    if depth > max_depth:
        return 0, [], ["RECURSION_DEPTH_EXCEEDED"]

    lookups = 0
    vendors = []
    warnings = []

    tokens = record.replace('"', '').split()
    for t in tokens:
        t_lower = t.lower()
        if t_lower == "ptr" or t_lower.startswith("ptr:"):
            lookups += 1
            warnings.append(f"DEPRECATED_PTR_MECHANISM ({t})")
        elif t_lower in ["a", "mx"] or t_lower.startswith("a:") or t_lower.startswith("mx:"):
            lookups += 1
        elif t_lower.startswith("include:") or t_lower.startswith("redirect="):
            lookups += 1
            target = t_lower.split(":", 1)[1] if ":" in t_lower else t_lower.split("=", 1)[1]
            
            # Map known vendors
            for v in ["google", "salesforce", "zendesk", "hubspot", "mailgun", "sendgrid", "mandrill", "zoho", "oracle", "constantcontact", "mailchimp", "pardot", "postmark", "mtasv"]:
                if v in target and v not in vendors:
                    vendors.append(v)

            if target in seen:
                warnings.append(f"CIRCULAR_INCLUDE_LOOP ({target})")
                continue
            seen.add(target)

            # Query child record
            child_txts = query_doh(target, "TXT")
            child_spf = [c for c in child_txts if "v=spf1" in c.lower()]
            if child_spf:
                child_lookups, child_vendors, child_warns = trace_recursive_spf(child_spf[0], depth + 1, max_depth, seen)
                lookups += child_lookups
                for cv in child_vendors:
                    if cv not in vendors:
                        vendors.append(cv)
                warnings.extend(child_warns)

    return lookups, vendors, warnings

def deep_subdomain_audit(domain: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    prefixes = [
        "mail", "email", "e", "em", "news", "send", "m", "app", "go", 
        "links", "notifications", "bounce", "track", "marketing", "api"
    ]
    subdomains = []
    max_sub_lookups = 0

    for p in prefixes:
        sub = f"{p}.{domain}"
        txts = query_doh(sub, "TXT")
        a_records = query_doh(sub, "A")
        cname_records = query_doh(sub, "CNAME")
        spf_rec = [t for t in txts if "v=spf1" in t.lower()]

        sub_info = {
            "subdomain": sub,
            "has_spf": bool(spf_rec),
            "spf_raw": spf_rec[0] if spf_rec else None,
            "lookups": 0,
            "warnings": [],
            "a": a_records,
            "cname": cname_records
        }

        if spf_rec:
            lookups, _, warns = trace_recursive_spf(spf_rec[0])
            sub_info["lookups"] = lookups
            sub_info["warnings"] = warns
            if lookups > max_sub_lookups:
                max_sub_lookups = lookups

        if spf_rec or a_records or cname_records:
            subdomains.append(sub_info)

    return {
        "count": len(subdomains),
        "max_subdomain_lookups": max_sub_lookups,
        "subdomains": subdomains
    }

def run_deterministic_pipeline(domain: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"TENSIX DEEP PIPELINE ENGINE: {domain.upper()}")
    print("=" * 80)

    # STAGE 1: Liveness Shield
    print("\n[STAGE 1/5] Executing Liveness & Reputation Shield...")
    is_live, op_weight, shield_meta = verify_live_website(domain, timeout=5)
    print(f"  Live: {is_live} | Operational Weight: {op_weight}/100 | Shield Reason: {shield_meta.get('reason')}")
    if not is_live:
        print("[-] Domain rejected at Stage 1. Halting pipeline.")
        return {"error": "Domain failed liveness shield"}

    # STAGE 2: Web Crawler (HTML /team, /about, /careers)
    print("\n[STAGE 2/5] Running Deep Web Page Extraction...")
    # Priority URLs
    extracted_emails = set()
    leadership = []
    base_url = f"https://{domain}"
    headers = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) TENSIX-Engine/6.0"}
    
    for path in ["/", "/about", "/team", "/contact", "/privacy-policy", "/terms", "/careers", "/jobs"]:
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(urllib.parse.urljoin(base_url, path), headers=headers)
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4) as resp:
                html = resp.read().decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
                found = re.findall(r'[a-zA-Z0-9_.+-]+@' + re.escape(domain), html, re.IGNORECASE)
                for f in found:
                    extracted_emails.add(f.lower())
                # Check for leadership
                for line in html.split("\n"):
                    if any(t in line.lower() for t in ["founder", "ceo", "chief technology officer", "vp of engineering"]):
                        c = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', line).strip()
                        if 5 < len(c) < 80 and c not in leadership:
                            leadership.append(c)
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"  Found Direct @{domain} Emails: {sorted(list(extracted_emails))}")
    print(f"  Extracted Leadership Titles: {leadership[:4]}")

    # STAGE 3: Recursive Subdomain & Route Asymmetry Recon
    print("\n[STAGE 3/5] Running Recursive Subdomain & Burner Audit...")
    sub_data = deep_subdomain_audit(domain)
    print(f"  Active Subdomains Discovered: {sub_data['count']}")
    print(f"  Max Subdomain Lookup Depth: {sub_data['max_subdomain_lookups']}")
    for s in sub_data["subdomains"]:
        if s["has_spf"]:
            print(f"    - {s['subdomain']}: {s['lookups']} Lookups | Warnings: {s['warnings']}")

    # STAGE 4: 34-Variable & Exact Weight Evaluation
    print("\n[STAGE 4/5] Evaluating Complete 34-Variable Telemetry Graph...")
    txts = query_doh(domain, "TXT")
    spf_records = [t for t in txts if "v=spf1" in t.lower()]
    dmarc_records = query_doh(f"_dmarc.{domain}", "TXT")
    dmarc_entry = [d for d in dmarc_records if "v=dmarc1" in d.lower()]

    score = 0
    vars_status = {}

    # VAR_01: Apex SPF Lookups (40 pts)
    apex_lookups = 0
    detected_vendors = []
    apex_warnings = []
    if spf_records:
        apex_lookups, detected_vendors, apex_warnings = trace_recursive_spf(spf_records[0])

    if apex_lookups > 10:
        score += 40
        vars_status["VAR_01"] = {"status": "FAILED", "weight": 40, "detail": f"{apex_lookups}/10 lookups (RFC 7208 PermError)"}
    else:
        vars_status["VAR_01"] = {"status": "PASS", "weight": 0, "detail": f"{apex_lookups}/10 lookups"}

    # VAR_03: DMARC Policy (30 pts)
    dmarc_val = "MISSING"
    if dmarc_entry:
        m = re.search(r"p=([a-zA-Z]+)", dmarc_entry[0], re.IGNORECASE)
        if m:
            dmarc_val = m.group(1).lower()

    if dmarc_val in ["none", "MISSING"]:
        score += 30
        vars_status["VAR_03"] = {"status": "VULNERABLE", "weight": 30, "detail": f"p={dmarc_val} (Unenforced)"}
    else:
        vars_status["VAR_03"] = {"status": "ENFORCED", "weight": 0, "detail": f"p={dmarc_val}"}

    # VAR_28 & VAR_29: Subdomain Route Asymmetry & Burner Disparity (20 pts)
    if sub_data["max_subdomain_lookups"] > 10:
        score += 25
        vars_status["VAR_28"] = {
            "status": "SUBDOMAIN_PERMERROR", 
            "weight": 25, 
            "detail": f"Subdomains blow ceiling at {sub_data['max_subdomain_lookups']} lookups (Looping/Deprecated mechanisms)"
        }
    elif apex_lookups > 8 and sub_data["count"] > 0:
        score += 20
        vars_status["VAR_28"] = {"status": "APEX_DRAG", "weight": 20, "detail": f"Apex carries {apex_lookups} lookups"}
    else:
        vars_status["VAR_28"] = {"status": "BALANCED", "weight": 0, "detail": "Subdomain and apex routing consistent"}

    # VAR_31: PTR / FCrDNS Alignment (25 pts)
    ptr_fail = False
    for s in sub_data["subdomains"]:
        if "mail." in s["subdomain"] and s["a"]:
            ip = s["a"][0]
            try:
                socket.gethostbyaddr(ip)
            except Exception:
                ptr_fail = True
                break
    if ptr_fail:
        score += 25
        vars_status["VAR_31"] = {"status": "FAILED", "weight": 25, "detail": f"Sending host lacks reverse PTR"}
    else:
        vars_status["VAR_31"] = {"status": "PASS", "weight": 0, "detail": "Verified or Cloud-hosted"}

    # VAR_32: MTA-STS (15 pts)
    mta_sts = query_doh(f"_mta-sts.{domain}", "TXT")
    if not mta_sts:
        score += 15
        vars_status["VAR_32"] = {"status": "MISSING", "weight": 15, "detail": "Missing _mta-sts policy (STARTTLS downgrade risk)"}
    else:
        vars_status["VAR_32"] = {"status": "ENFORCED", "weight": 0, "detail": "MTA-STS Active"}

    # VAR_34: RUF Reporting (10 pts)
    if dmarc_entry and "ruf=" not in dmarc_entry[0].lower():
        score += 10
        vars_status["VAR_34"] = {"status": "MISSING_RUF", "weight": 10, "detail": "No real-time forensic failure reporting"}
    else:
        vars_status["VAR_34"] = {"status": "PASS", "weight": 0, "detail": "RUF active"}

    bleed_score = min(score, 100)
    print(f"  TOTAL BLEED SCORE: {bleed_score}/100 | PRIORITY: {bleed_score >= 60}")
    print(f"  Apex Lookups: {apex_lookups}/10 | Subdomain Max: {sub_data['max_subdomain_lookups']}/10")
    print(f"  Detected Vendors: {detected_vendors}")

    # STAGE 5: Pre-Flight Socket Handshake RCPT Verifier
    print("\n[STAGE 5/5] Executing Pre-Flight Socket Handshake Verification...")
    verified_mailboxes = []
    
    candidates = list(extracted_emails)
    for def_email in [f"contact@{domain}", f"founders@{domain}", f"info@{domain}"]:
        if def_email not in candidates:
            candidates.append(def_email)

    for cand in candidates[:4]:
        check = verify_email_comprehensive(cand)
        print(f"  {cand:<32} -> Deliverable: {check.get('is_deliverable')} | Bounce Risk: {check.get('bounce_risk')} | Code: {check.get('status_code')}")
        if check.get("is_deliverable"):
            verified_mailboxes.append({"email": cand, "check": check})

    dossier = {
        "domain": domain,
        "is_live": is_live,
        "bleed_score": bleed_score,
        "is_priority_lead": bleed_score >= 60,
        "apex_lookups": apex_lookups,
        "max_subdomain_lookups": sub_data["max_subdomain_lookups"],
        "dmarc_policy": dmarc_val,
        "detected_vendors": detected_vendors,
        "variables": vars_status,
        "subdomains": sub_data["subdomains"],
        "extracted_emails": list(extracted_emails),
        "verified_mailboxes": verified_mailboxes,
        "leadership_mentions": leadership[:5]
    }

    out_file = os.path.join(r"D:\projects\HK\tensix\data", f"pipeline_audit_{domain}.json")
    with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(dossier, f, indent=2)

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print(f"[+] COMPLETE DETERMINISTIC AUDIT SAVED TO: {out_file}")
    print(f"[+] ZERO EMAILS DISPATCHED. READY FOR HUMAN COMMAND.")
    print("=" * 80)

    return dossier

if __name__ == "__main__":
    target = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "adjust.com"
    run_deterministic_pipeline(target)

FILE: crawl_betalist.py

import urllib.request
import ssl
import json
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
import sys

sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')

def crawl_betalist_full():
    url = "https://betalist.com/"
    headers = {
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/122.0.0.0 Safari/537.36'
    }
    
    ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
    ctx.check_hostname = False
    ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE
    
    print(f"[+] Fetching live feed from {url}...")
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, context=ctx, timeout=15) as resp:
        html = resp.read().decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')
        
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, 'html.parser')
    
    # Each startup row has class 'startup-row'
    rows = soup.find_all(class_=lambda c: c and 'startup-row' in c)
    print(f"[+] Found {len(rows)} startup entries on BetaList.")
    
    startups = []
    seen = set()
    
    for r in rows:
        link = r.find('a', href=lambda h: h and h.startswith('/startups/'))
        if not link:
            continue
            
        href = link.get('href')
        slug = href.replace('/startups/', '').strip('/')
        
        if slug in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(slug)
        
        # Extract title and pitch text
        # Usually inside div text
        texts = [t.strip() for t in r.stripped_strings if t.strip()]
        
        name = slug.replace('-', ' ').title()
        pitch = ""
        
        if len(texts) >= 1:
            name = texts[0]
        if len(texts) >= 2:
            pitch = ' '.join(texts[1:])
            
        startups.append({
            "name": name,
            "slug": slug,
            "betalist_url": f"https://betalist.com{href}",
            "pitch": pitch
        })
        
    out_file = r'D:\projects\HK\crawler\betalist_startups.json'
    with open(out_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as fp:
        json.dump(startups, fp, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        
    print(f"[+] Successfully extracted {len(startups)} startups!")
    print(f"[+] Output saved to: {out_file}\n")
    
    print(f"{'#':<3} | {'STARTUP NAME':<25} | {'PITCH / DESCRIPTION'}")
    print("-" * 90)
    for i, s in enumerate(startups, 1):
        print(f"{i:<3} | {s['name'][:24]:<25} | {s['pitch'][:55]}")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    crawl_betalist_full()

================================================================================
FILE: crawl_global_ashby.py
================================================================================

import urllib.request
import json

SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS = [
    "systems", "infrastructure", "distributed", "kernel", "network", 
    "engine", "runtime", "storage", "database", "rust", "c++", "go", "golang",
    "core", "platform", "backend", "performance", "sre", "data engineering",
    "observability", "scale", "telemetry", "streaming", "pipeline"
]

boards = [
    ("PostHog", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/posthog"),
    ("Resend", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/resend"),
    ("QuickNode", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/quicknode"),
    ("Tavily", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/tavily"),
    ("LangChain", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/langchain"),
    ("Dagger", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/dagger"),
    ("Neon", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/neon"),
    ("LlamaIndex", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/llamaindex"),
    ("Chroma", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/chroma"),
    ("Together AI", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/togetherai"),
    ("Anyscale", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/anyscale"),
    ("Modal", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/modal"),
    ("Baseten", "https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/baseten")
]

found = []
print("=== Crawling Global Tier-1 Systems & Infra (Ashby) ===")
for name, url in boards:
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
            roles = []
            for j in data.get("jobs", []):
                t = j.get("title", "")
                if any(k in t.lower() for k in SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS):
                    roles.append({"title": t, "location": j.get("locationName", "Remote"), "url": j.get("jobUrl", "")})
            if roles:
                print(f"[+] {name}: {len(roles)} systems/infra roles")
                found.append({"company": name, "roles": roles})

import json
import ssl

ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
ctx.check_hostname = False
ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE

headers = {
    "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36",
    "Accept": "application/json, text/plain, */*"
}

            jobs = data.get("jobs", [])
            for j in jobs:

                loc = j.get("location", {}).get("name", "India")

                        "board": "greenhouse",
                        "title": title,
                        "location": loc,
                        "url": j.get("absolute_url", "")
                    })
    except Exception:
        pass

# Crawl Lever
for name, slug in lever_slugs:
    url = f"https://api.lever.co/v0/postings/{slug}?mode=json"
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4, context=ctx) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
            for j in data:
                title = j.get("text", "")
                loc = j.get("categories", {}).get("location", "India")
                if any(k in title.lower() for k in SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS):
                    all_harvested.append({
                        "company": name,
                        "board": "lever",
                        "title": title,
                        "location": loc,
                        "url": j.get("hostedUrl", "")
                    })
    except Exception:
        pass

# Crawl Ashby
for name, slug in ashby_slugs:
    url = f"https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/{slug}"
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4, context=ctx) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
            for j in data.get("jobs", []):
                title = j.get("title", "")
                loc = j.get("locationName", "India / Remote")
                if any(k in title.lower() for k in SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS):
                    all_harvested.append({
                        "company": name,
                        "board": "ashby",
                        "title": title,
                        "location": loc,
                        "url": j.get("jobUrl", "")
                    })
    except Exception:
        pass

# Deduplicate
seen = set()
unique_results = []
for j in all_harvested:
    key = (j["company"], j["title"], j["location"])
    if key not in seen:
        seen.add(key)
        unique_results.append(j)

out_file = r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\massive_indian_jobs_harvest.json"
with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(unique_results, f, indent=2)

print(f"\n[MASSIVE CRAWL COMPLETE] Extracted {len(unique_results)} active Indian systems/infra engineering roles.")
by_comp = {}
for j in unique_results:
    c = j["company"]
    by_comp[c] = by_comp.get(c, 0) + 1

for c, count in sorted(by_comp.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True):
    print(f"  {c:<20}: {count} roles")

================================================================================
FILE: crawler.py
================================================================================

"""
crawlerr: Minimalist Zero-Dependency High-Throughput Crawler & Intent Pipeline
Philosophy: Ponytail Mode (Stdlib only, zero-boilerplate, deterministic DSA)
"""

import sys
import json
import hashlib
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.parse
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from collections import deque
from typing import Set, Dict, List, Optional

class SimpleLinkExtractor(HTMLParser):
    """Zero-dependency HTML link and text parser (DSA: Linear state-machine scanner)."""
    def __init__(self, base_url: str):
        super().__init__()
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.links: List[str] = []
        self.text_chunks: List[str] = []

    def handle_starttag(self, tag: str, attrs: list):
        if tag == "a":
            for k, v in attrs:
                if k == "href" and v:
                    absolute_url = urllib.parse.urljoin(self.base_url, v)
                    if absolute_url.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
                        self.links.append(absolute_url.split("#")[0])

    def handle_data(self, data: str):
        cleaned = data.strip()
        if cleaned:
            self.text_chunks.append(cleaned)

class CrawlerEngine:
    """
    DSA Core:
    - Queue (BFS Traversal): O(1) dequeue via collections.deque
    - Hash Table (Deduplication): O(1) membership check using SHA-256 signatures
    """
    def __init__(self, max_depth: int = 2, max_pages: int = 20):
        self.max_depth = max_depth
        self.max_pages = max_pages
        self.seen_signatures: Set[str] = set()
        self.results: List[Dict] = []
        self.queue: deque = deque()

    @staticmethod
    def compute_signature(content: str) -> str:
        """Deterministic fingerprinting (DSA: Cryptographic Hash for exact idempotency)."""
        return hashlib.sha256(content.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

    def fetch_url(self, url: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Fetch URL with stdlib timeout & browser headers."""
        headers = {
            "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) crawlerr/1.0"
        }
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as resp:
                if "text/html" in resp.headers.get("Content-Type", ""):
                    return resp.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        except Exception:
            return None
        return None

    def run(self, seed_url: str):
        print(f"[*] Starting crawlerr on: {seed_url}")
        self.queue.append((seed_url, 0))

        while self.queue and len(self.results) < self.max_pages:
            current_url, depth = self.queue.popleft()
            if depth > self.max_depth:
                continue

            html = self.fetch_url(current_url)
            if not html:
                continue

            parser = SimpleLinkExtractor(current_url)
            parser.feed(html)

            page_text = " ".join(parser.text_chunks)
            sig = self.compute_signature(page_text)

            # Deduplication boundary check
            if sig in self.seen_signatures:
                continue
            self.seen_signatures.add(sig)

            print(f"[+] [{len(self.results) + 1}/{self.max_pages}] Scraped: {current_url} (depth={depth})")
            self.results.append({
                "url": current_url,
                "depth": depth,
                "signature": sig,
                "text_snippet": page_text[:300],
                "discovered_links": len(parser.links)
            })

            # Enqueue child links (BFS)
            if depth < self.max_depth:
                for link in parser.links[:10]:
                    self.queue.append((link, depth + 1))

        print(f"[OK] Completed crawl. Total unique entities indexed: {len(self.results)}")
        return self.results

def self_check():
    """Ponytail single runnable self-check (no test framework required)."""
    h = SimpleLinkExtractor("https://example.com")
    h.feed('<a href="/test">link</a><p>hello world</p>')
    assert "https://example.com/test" in h.links, "Link extraction failed"
    assert "hello world" in h.text_chunks, "Text extraction failed"
    sig = CrawlerEngine.compute_signature("hello world")
    assert len(sig) == 64, "SHA-256 signature length mismatch"
    print("[PASS] Self-check verified successfully.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    self_check()
    target = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "https://news.ycombinator.com"
    crawler = CrawlerEngine(max_depth=1, max_pages=5)
    crawler.run(target)


================================================================================
FILE: curl_all_handbook.py
================================================================================

import json
import subprocess
import os
import re
from html.parser import HTMLParser

class TextExtractor(HTMLParser):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.texts = []
        self.ignore = False

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        if tag in ["script", "style", "nav", "svg"]:
            self.ignore = True

    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        if tag in ["script", "style", "nav", "svg"]:
            self.ignore = False

    def handle_data(self, data):
        if not self.ignore:
            cleaned = data.strip()
            if cleaned:
                self.texts.append(cleaned)

def extract_main_text(html: str) -> str:
    parser = TextExtractor()
    parser.feed(html)
    return " ".join(parser.texts)

with open(r"D:\projects\HK\crawler\handbook_links.json", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    links = json.load(f)

output_dir = r"D:\projects\HK\crawler\resend_handbook_dump"
os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)

print(f"Executing curl.exe on {len(links)} Resend Handbook links...")

summary_manifest = []

for idx, rel_path in enumerate(links, 1):
    full_url = f"https://resend.com{rel_path}"
    slug = rel_path.strip("/").replace("/", "_")
    target_file = os.path.join(output_dir, f"{slug}.txt")
    
    cmd = ["curl.exe", "-sL", full_url]
    try:
        raw_html = subprocess.check_output(cmd, text=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=15)
        clean_text = extract_main_text(raw_html)
        
        # Save readable text dump
        with open(target_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f_out:
            f_out.write(f"URL: {full_url}\n")
            f_out.write("=" * 60 + "\n\n")
            f_out.write(clean_text)
            
        summary_manifest.append({
            "index": idx,
            "url": full_url,
            "path": target_file,
            "char_count": len(clean_text),
            "snippet": clean_text[:200]
        })
        print(f"[{idx:02d}/{len(links)}] Curled: {full_url} ({len(clean_text)} chars)")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[{idx:02d}/{len(links)}] FAILED: {full_url} - {e}")

manifest_file = os.path.join(output_dir, "manifest.json")
with open(manifest_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(summary_manifest, f, indent=2)

print(f"\nALL 65 LINKS CURLED SUCCESSFULLY. Manifest written to {manifest_file}")

    domain = target["domain"]

    try:
        if ats == "ashby":
            url = f"https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/{slug}"
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as resp:
                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                for j in data.get("jobs", []):
                    title = j.get("title", "")
                    if any(k in title.lower() for k in SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS):
                        roles.append({
                            "title": title,
                            "location": j.get("locationName", "Remote"),
                            "url": j.get("jobUrl", "")
                        })
        elif ats == "greenhouse":
            url = f"https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/{slug}/jobs"
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})

                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                for j in data.get("jobs", []):
                    title = j.get("title", "")
                    if any(k in title.lower() for k in SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS):
                        roles.append({
                            "title": title,
                            "location": j.get("location", {}).get("name", "Remote"),
                            "url": j.get("absolute_url", "")
                        })
        if roles:
            print(f"[+] {comp}: Found {len(roles)} systems engineering positions")
            discovered.append({"company": comp, "domain": domain, "roles": roles})
    except Exception:
        pass

out_path = r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\deep_systems_crawl_pool.json"

FILE: deep_yoe_crawler.py

import os
import sys
import json
import urllib.request
import urllib.parse
from job_filter import is_hard_blocked_job, parse_years_of_experience

COMPANIES = [
    {"name": "Resend", "ats": "ashby", "slug": "resend", "domain": "resend.com"},
    {"name": "Modal", "ats": "ashby", "slug": "modal", "domain": "modal.com"},
    {"name": "Railway", "ats": "ashby", "slug": "railway", "domain": "railway.app"},
    {"name": "LiveKit", "ats": "ashby", "slug": "livekit", "domain": "livekit.io"},
    {"name": "Warp", "ats": "ashby", "slug": "warp", "domain": "warp.dev"},
    {"name": "Supabase", "ats": "greenhouse", "slug": "supabase", "domain": "supabase.com"},
    {"name": "Vercel", "ats": "greenhouse", "slug": "vercel", "domain": "vercel.com"},
    {"name": "CockroachLabs", "ats": "greenhouse", "slug": "cockroachlabs", "domain": "cockroachlabs.com"},
    {"name": "Timescale", "ats": "greenhouse", "slug": "timescale", "domain": "timescale.com"},
    {"name": "ScyllaDB", "ats": "greenhouse", "slug": "scylladb", "domain": "scylladb.com"}
]

SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS = [
    "systems", "infrastructure", "distributed", "kernel", "network", 
    "engine", "runtime", "storage", "database", "rust", "c++", "go",
    "backend", "platform"
]

validated_jobs = []

print("=== Running Deep Body YoE & Title Filter Crawl ===")

for comp in COMPANIES:
    if len(validated_jobs) >= 20:
        break
    ats = comp["ats"]
    slug = comp["slug"]
    name = comp["name"]
    domain = comp["domain"]
    
    try:
        if ats == "ashby":
            url = f"https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/{slug}"
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=6) as resp:
                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                for j in data.get("jobs", []):
                    title = j.get("title", "")
                    desc = j.get("descriptionHtml", "")
                    
                    if not any(k in title.lower() for k in SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS):
                        continue
                        
                    blocked, reason = is_hard_blocked_job(title, desc, max_allowed_yoe=4)
                    if blocked:
                        # Log filtered job
                        # print(f"  [BLOCKED] {name} - {title}: {reason}")
                        continue
                        
                    yoe = parse_years_of_experience(desc)
                    validated_jobs.append({
                        "company": name,
                        "domain": domain,
                        "title": title,
                        "required_yoe": yoe if yoe is not None else "Unspecified / Open",
                        "location": j.get("locationName", "Remote"),
                        "url": j.get("jobUrl", "")
                    })
                    if len(validated_jobs) >= 20:
                        break
                        
        elif ats == "greenhouse":
            # For Greenhouse, fetch individual job description content
            url = f"https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/{slug}/jobs"
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=6) as resp:
                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                for j in data.get("jobs", []):
                    title = j.get("title", "")
                    if not any(k in title.lower() for k in SYSTEMS_KEYWORDS):
                        continue
                        
                    # Fetch detailed body for greenhouse job
                    job_id = j.get("id")
                    detail_url = f"https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/{slug}/jobs/{job_id}"
                    req_detail = urllib.request.Request(detail_url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
                    try:
                        with urllib.request.urlopen(req_detail, timeout=5) as detail_resp:
                            detail_data = json.loads(detail_resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                            desc = detail_data.get("content", "")
                    except Exception:
                        desc = ""
                        
                    blocked, reason = is_hard_blocked_job(title, desc, max_allowed_yoe=4)
                    if blocked:
                        continue
                        
                    yoe = parse_years_of_experience(desc)
                    validated_jobs.append({
                        "company": name,
                        "domain": domain,
                        "title": title,
                        "required_yoe": yoe if yoe is not None else "Unspecified / Open",
                        "location": j.get("location", {}).get("name", "Remote"),
                        "url": j.get("absolute_url", "")
                    })
                    if len(validated_jobs) >= 20:
                        break
    except Exception as e:
        pass

out_path = r"D:\projects\HK\crawler\clean_validated_jobs.json"
with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(validated_jobs, f, indent=2)

print(f"DONE: Harvested {len(validated_jobs)} clean jobs with STRICT YoE <= 4 or Unspecified.")

FILE: discover_indian_slugs.py

import urllib.request
import json
import re

headers = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)"}

def check_greenhouse_slug(slug):
    url = f"https://boards-api.greenhouse.io/v1/boards/{slug}/jobs"
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
            return len(data.get("jobs", []))
    except Exception:
        return None

def check_lever_slug(slug):
    url = f"https://api.lever.co/v0/postings/{slug}?mode=json"
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
            return len(data)
    except Exception:
        return None

def check_ashby_slug(slug):
    url = f"https://api.ashbyhq.com/posting-api/job-board/{slug}"
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
            return len(data.get("jobs", []))
    except Exception:
        return None

candidates = [
    "postman", "postmaninc", "postmanlabs",
    "hasura", "hasurainc",
    "browserstack",
    "razorpay", "razorpaysoftware",
    "swiggy", "bundl",
    "clevertap", "wizrocket",
    "chargebee",
    "khatabook",
    "groww",
    "zerodha", "kite",
    "juspay", "juspaytech",
    "infracloud", "infracloudio",
    "delhivery",
    "urbancompany", "urbanclap",
    "meesho",
    "dream11", "dreamsports",
    "unacademy",
    "ola", "olacabs", "olakrutrim", "krutrim",
    "lenskart",
    "shadowfax",
    "spinny",
    "cars24",
    "zepto",
    "blinkit", "grofers"
]

print("=== Probing ATS Slugs for Indian Scaleups ===")
for c in candidates:
    gh = check_greenhouse_slug(c)
    if gh is not None:
        print(f"[FOUND Greenhouse] {c}: {gh} total jobs")
    lv = check_lever_slug(c)
    if lv is not None:
        print(f"[FOUND Lever] {c}: {lv} total jobs")
    ash = check_ashby_slug(c)
    if ash is not None:
        print(f"[FOUND Ashby] {c}: {ash} total jobs")

print("Slug probe complete.")

    }

def dispatch_all():
    print(f"Connecting to Brevo SMTP via {login}...")
    try:
        with smtplib.SMTP(smtp_server, smtp_port, timeout=15) as server:
            server.ehlo()
            server.starttls()
            server.ehlo()
            server.login(login, key)
            
            for item in followups:
                msg = MIMEMultipart("alternative")
                msg["Subject"] = item["subject"]
                msg["From"] = "Hemal Shah <hemal@tensix.in>"
                msg["To"] = ", ".join(item["to"])
                msg["Reply-To"] = "hemal@tensix.in"
                
                part1 = MIMEText(item["text"], "plain", "utf-8")
                part2 = MIMEText(item["html"], "html", "utf-8")
                msg.attach(part1)
                msg.attach(part2)
                
                server.sendmail("hemal@tensix.in", item["to"], msg.as_string())
                print(f"SUCCESS: Delivered humanized follow-up to {item['name']} -> {', '.join(item['to'])}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"FAILED during dispatch: {e}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    dispatch_all()

FILE: dispatch_thread_followup.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
TENSIX High-Conversion Thread Follow-Up Dispatcher
Fires targeted closing follow-ups into existing email threads for:
1. Anand Jain & Sunil Thomas (CleverTap)
2. Vijay Yalamanchili (Keka HR)
3. Raviteja Dodda (MoEngage)
4. Team @ SketchDeck

Strictly uses Brevo SMTP with RFC 5321 pre-flight validation.
Zero free technical sauce, pure revenue leak and pipeline closing pressure.
"""

import sys
import smtplib
import os
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart

sys.path.append(r"D:\projects\HK\crawler")
from run_production_engine import load_env
from smtp_rcpt_verifier import verify_mailbox_rcpt

def send_thread_followup(to_email, cc_emails, subject, text_content, html_content):
    config = load_env()
    smtp_login = config.get("BREVO_SMTP_LOGIN")
    smtp_key = config.get("BREVO_SMTP_KEY")
    sender_email = config.get("SENDER_EMAIL", "hemal@tensix.in")
    sender_name = config.get("SENDER_NAME", "Hemal Shah | TENSIX")

    all_recipients = [to_email] + cc_emails
    verified_recipients = [r for r in all_recipients if verify_mailbox_rcpt(r)]

    if not verified_recipients:
        print(f"[-] [BOUNCE SHIELD] All recipients failed pre-flight verification: {all_recipients}")
        return False

    msg = MIMEMultipart("alternative")
    msg["From"] = f"{sender_name} <{sender_email}>"
    msg["To"] = to_email
    if cc_emails:
        msg["Cc"] = ", ".join(cc_emails)
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg.attach(MIMEText(text_content, "plain"))
    msg.attach(MIMEText(html_content, "html"))

    try:
        server = smtplib.SMTP("smtp-relay.brevo.com", 587)
        server.starttls()
        server.login(smtp_login, smtp_key)
        server.sendmail(sender_email, verified_recipients, msg.as_string())
        server.quit()
        print(f"[+] [SUCCESS] Dispatched thread follow-up to: {verified_recipients}")
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[-] [FAILED] {e}")
        return False

def follow_up_clevertap():
    to = "anand@clevertap.com"

Your reps assume the buyer ghosted them after the demo. In reality, the proposal never made it past the recipient's firewall. You're spending tens of thousands acquiring enterprise pipeline, only to drop deals at the final yard.

We specialize in bulletproofing deal-closing infrastructure and high-ticket deliverability for scaling enterprise platforms.

If this isn't on your radar right now, no worries at all and I'll close the file.

Hemal Shah
Infrastructure & MailOps | TENSIX
https://tensix.in

    html = """<!DOCTYPE html>

import json

path = r"D:\projects\emaillinggggggg\crawler\indian_infra_jobs.json"
with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    data = json.load(f)

for item in data:

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
TENSIX Multi-Dimensional Dubai Scaleup Audit Engine
Audits the top 8 Dubai/UAE unicorns & scaleups across:
1. MailOps & RFC Transport (SPF Lookups, DMARC, Vendor Sprawl)
2. AEO & Agent Readiness (RFC 8288 Link headers, robots.txt AI crawler tokens, llms.txt)
3. SEO Knowledge Graph (JSON-LD 1.1 AST, Wikidata QIDs, Schema.org Organization)
Synthesizes multi-dimensional commercial attack vectors ($350 Triage -> $1,200 AEO -> $1,500/mo Retainer).
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import re
import urllib.request
import urllib.parse
import ssl

sys.path.append(r"D:\projects\HK\crawler")
from mailops_scorer import score_and_package_domain
from reputation_shield import verify_live_website

DUBAI_TARGETS = [
    {"domain": "dubizzle.com", "name": "Dubizzle Group", "sector": "Classifieds & Auto Unicorn"},
    {"domain": "kitopi.com", "name": "Kitopi", "sector": "Cloud Kitchen / FoodTech Unicorn"},
    {"domain": "postpay.io", "name": "Postpay", "sector": "UAE FinTech / BNPL"},
    {"domain": "lean.tech", "name": "Lean Technologies", "sector": "DIFC Open Banking API"},
    {"domain": "ziina.com", "name": "Ziina", "sector": "Dubai P2P & Merchant Payments"},
    {"domain": "baraka.co", "name": "Baraka", "sector": "Dubai Retail Investment Platform"},
    {"domain": "huspy.com", "name": "Huspy", "sector": "Dubai PropTech & Mortgages"}
]

def audit_aeo_and_seo(domain: str):
    """
    Evaluates:
    - RFC 8288 Link headers for agent discovery
    - RFC 9309 robots.txt permissions for ClaudeBot, GPTBot, PerplexityBot
    - JSON-LD structured data presence & Schema types
    """
    ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
    ctx.check_hostname = False
    ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE

    results = {
        "has_llms_txt": False,
        "robots_ai_status": "RESTRICTIVE_OR_DEFAULT",
        "has_json_ld": False,
        "schema_types": [],
        "aeo_score": 0
    }

    # 1. Check llms.txt
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(f"https://{domain}/llms.txt", headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4, context=ctx) as resp:
            if resp.getcode() == 200 and len(resp.read(500)) > 50:
                results["has_llms_txt"] = True
                results["aeo_score"] += 30
    except:
        pass

    # 2. Check robots.txt for AI bots
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(f"https://{domain}/robots.txt", headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4, context=ctx) as resp:
            content = resp.read().decode("utf-8", errors="ignore").lower()
            if "claudebot" in content or "gptbot" in content or "perplexitybot" in content:
                if "disallow: /" in content:
                    results["robots_ai_status"] = "BLOCKED_AI_AGENTS"
                else:
                    results["robots_ai_status"] = "EXPLICIT_AI_RULES"
                    results["aeo_score"] += 25
            else:
                results["robots_ai_status"] = "GENERIC_DEFAULT"
                results["aeo_score"] += 10
    except:
        pass

    # 3. Check Homepage HTML for JSON-LD Schemas
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(f"https://{domain}", headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5, context=ctx) as resp:
            html = resp.read(60000).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
            json_ld_matches = re.findall(r'<script[^>]+type=["\']application/ld\+json["\'][^>]*>(.*?)</script>', html, re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE)
            if json_ld_matches:
                results["has_json_ld"] = True
                results["aeo_score"] += 25
                for block in json_ld_matches[:5]:
                    types = re.findall(r'["\']@type["\']\s*:\s*["\']([^"\']+)["\']', block)
                    results["schema_types"].extend(types)
                results["schema_types"] = list(set(results["schema_types"]))
                if any(t in results["schema_types"] for t in ["Organization", "FinancialProduct", "FAQPage"]):
                    results["aeo_score"] += 20
    except:
        pass

    return results

def run_dubai_multidimensional_audit():
    print("=" * 85)
    print("TENSIX MULTI-DIMENSIONAL DUBAI AUDIT: MailOps + AEO + SEO Knowledge Graph")
    print("=" * 85)

    full_dossier = []

    for t in DUBAI_TARGETS:
        domain = t["domain"]
        print(f"\n---> Auditing: {t['name']} ({domain}) - {t['sector']}")

        # 1. MailOps RFC Scan
        mailops = score_and_package_domain(domain, biz_type="Dubai Tech Scaleup")
        
        # 2. AEO & SEO Knowledge Graph Scan
        aeo = audit_aeo_and_seo(domain)

        composite = {
            "target": t,
            "mailops": {
                "bleed_score": mailops["bleed_score"],
                "spf_lookups": mailops["spf_lookups"],
                "dmarc_policy": mailops["dmarc_policy"],
                "vendors": mailops["detected_vendors"]
            },
            "aeo_seo": aeo
        }
        full_dossier.append(composite)

        print(f"     [MAILOPS]: Bleed Score {mailops['bleed_score']}/100 | SPF: {mailops['spf_lookups']}/10 | DMARC: {mailops['dmarc_policy']}")
        print(f"     [AEO/SEO]: Score {aeo['aeo_score']}/100 | llms.txt: {aeo['has_llms_txt']} | JSON-LD: {aeo['has_json_ld']} ({', '.join(aeo['schema_types'][:3])})")
        print(f"     [AI BOT POLICY]: {aeo['robots_ai_status']}")

    out_file = r"D:\projects\HK\tensix\data\dubai_multidimensional_dossier.json"
    with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(full_dossier, f, indent=2)

    print("\n" + "=" * 85)
    print(f"[+] Multi-Dimensional Dubai Audit Complete! Saved to: {out_file}")
    print("=" * 85)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_dubai_multidimensional_audit()
